<h3 data-end="3089" data-section-id="3u3ds5" data-start="3076">&nbsp;</h3>

<h3 data-end="3089" data-section-id="3u3ds5" data-start="3076"><strong>FIDE ID for U12- U18</strong></h3>

<ul>
	<li>Valid <strong data-end="3118" data-start="3098">FIDE ID required</strong> for FIDE rating.</li>
	<li>If you have <u><strong>no FIDE ID and</strong></u></li>
	<li>you are Non-citizen/non-PR players/ NZ citizens- You&nbsp;<span style="color:#8e44ad;"><em><u><strong>must obtain FIDE ID via home federation be eligible to play</strong></u></em></span></li>
	<li>you are citizen/ PR players- we will register you with Australian Chess Federation</li>
	<li>If you are not sure, immediately email use at major_tournaments@hobsonsbaychess.com.&nbsp;</li>
</ul>
